/* 这些是负向选择器(隐藏"除当前分组以外"的面板),所以某个面板不渲染时对应
   那行只是匹配不到东西,不会出问题。account 和 voice 两行在纯前端版里用不上
   (面板和入口都被 backendFeaturesEnabled 摘掉了),但接后端的构建仍然需要,
   故保留。
   反过来的方向才危险:如果某个 view 还能被 setView 设到、而它的面板没了,
   home list 会被上面那条规则隐藏、面板又不存在 —— 整页空白。改这里时务必
   和 App.tsx 里的入口按钮成对检查。view 本身是 useState('main') 不持久化,
   所以不存在残留旧值的情况。 */
.screen--settings-app .settings-page[data-settings-view='account'] [data-settings-group]:not([data-settings-group='account']),
.screen--settings-app .settings-page[data-settings-view='model'] [data-settings-group]:not([data-settings-group='model']),
.screen--settings-app .settings-page[data-settings-view='voice'] [data-settings-group]:not([data-settings-group='voice']),
.screen--settings-app .settings-page[data-settings-view='memory'] [data-settings-group]:not([data-settings-group='memory']),
.screen--settings-app .settings-page[data-settings-view='files'] [data-settings-group]:not([data-settings-group='files']),
.screen--settings-app .settings-page[data-settings-view='events'] [data-settings-group]:not([data-settings-group='events']),
.screen--settings-app .settings-page[data-settings-view='proactive'] [data-settings-group]:not([data-settings-group='proactive']),
.screen--settings-app .settings-page[data-settings-view='proactiveAdvanced'] [data-settings-group]:not([data-settings-group='proactive']),
.screen--settings-app .settings-page[data-settings-view='contactAdvanced'] [data-settings-group]:not([data-settings-group='files']),
.screen--settings-app .settings-page[data-settings-view='data'] [data-settings-group]:not([data-settings-group='data']),

   white/gray surfaces and typography, while red is reserved for state/action. */

/* iOS zooms focused text controls whose computed font size is below 16px.
   Keep zoom available for accessibility, but do not trigger it as a side
   effect of entering a key, URL, search term or other form value. */
html.platform-ios input:not([type]),
html.platform-ios input[type='text'],
html.platform-ios input[type='password'],
html.platform-ios input[type='email'],
html.platform-ios input[type='url'],

html.platform-ios input[type='number'],
html.platform-ios input[type='date'],
html.platform-ios input[type='time'],
html.platform-ios input[type='datetime-local'],
html.platform-ios input[type='month'],
html.platform-ios input[type='week'],
html.platform-ios textarea,
html.platform-ios select {
  font-size: 16px !important;
}

/* Memory storage fault bar (shell layer).
   Deliberately not .top-toast: that one is cleared by a 5s timer, which is right
   for a transient write error and wrong for a fault the user must act on. This
   bar stays until the fault is resolved. Positioned to match .top-toast so the
   two read as the same family. */
.memory-storage-notice {
  position: absolute;
  top: calc(62px + var(--ios-safe-top-offset));
  left: 50%;
  /* Below every dialog (.confirm-overlay is 5, the rest run 20-200). At 7 this bar
     outranked .confirm-overlay and covered it, while still losing to the higher
     overlays — visibly inconsistent. A standing status bar must never cover a
     dialog the user is trying to answer. */
  z-index: 4;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  max-width: calc(100% - 40px);
  padding: 9px 10px 9px 16px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: oklch(0.32 0.14 28 / 0.96);
  color: oklch(0.99 0.001 250);
  font-size: 12.5px;
  font-weight: 650;
  box-shadow: 0 12px 24px oklch(0.15 0.004 250 / 0.2);
  transform: translateX(-50%);
}

/* Both can be visible at once: the toast is transient, this bar is not. Offset
   only when the toast is actually rendered, so the common case stays aligned. */
.top-toast + .memory-storage-notice {
  top: calc(106px + var(--ios-safe-top-offset));
}

.memory-storage-notice__reason {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  line-height: 1.35;
}

.memory-storage-notice__action {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  padding: 5px 13px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: oklch(0.99 0.001 250 / 0.94);
  color: oklch(0.3 0.13 28);
  font-size: 12.5px;
  font-weight: 750;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.memory-storage-notice__action:active {
  background: oklch(0.93 0.004 250 / 0.94);
}

/* Scheduled-deletion bar. Same slim shape and z-index reasoning as
   .memory-storage-notice: it must stay visible without ever covering a dialog. */
.pending-deletion-notice {
  position: absolute;
  top: calc(62px + var(--ios-safe-top-offset));
  left: 50%;
  z-index: 4;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  max-width: calc(100% - 40px);
  padding: 9px 10px 9px 16px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  color: oklch(0.99 0.001 250);
  font-size: 12.5px;
  font-weight: 650;
  box-shadow: 0 12px 24px oklch(0.15 0.004 250 / 0.2);
  transform: translateX(-50%);
}

/* Both bars can be present. Stack rather than overlap, and keep the storage
   fault on top since it is actionable right now. */
.memory-storage-notice + .pending-deletion-notice {
  top: calc(106px + var(--ios-safe-top-offset));
}

.top-toast + .pending-deletion-notice {
  top: calc(106px + var(--ios-safe-top-offset));
}

/* Days out: informative, not alarming. Slate blue rather than red so the bar does
   not cry wolf for a week and get tuned out. */
.pending-deletion-notice--calm {
  background: oklch(0.42 0.06 250 / 0.96);
}

/* Under three days: amber. Something the user should decide on soon. */
.pending-deletion-notice--warning {
  background: oklch(0.46 0.12 65 / 0.96);
}

/* Final day: matches the storage-fault red, the app's established "act now". */
.pending-deletion-notice--critical {
  background: oklch(0.32 0.14 28 / 0.96);
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference) {
  /* Only the last day pulses, and slowly. A permanent animation for seven days
     would be noise; on the final day it is the point. */
  .pending-deletion-notice--critical {
    animation: pending-deletion-pulse 2.4s ease-in-out infinite;
  }
}

/* Memory recovery panel (memory app).
   Replaces the old "请刷新页面后重试" toast, which contradicted the shell bar: a
   write failure leaves reads working, so "cannot read safely" was wrong and
   refreshing does not free disk space. This is a panel rather than a toast because
   it carries steps and actions the user has to work through. */
